# Top Hand Studio — content inventory

Captured 2026-11-02. Phase 1 archive; no replacement-site implementation.

**Coverage:** 58 normalized public URLs, 35 print listings plus unresolved ANC, 155 downloaded image URLs. Homepage links, recursive same-site content links, all observed pagination and all 46 sitemap entries were checked. Query variants are mapped below; accounts/cart/checkout are recorded but not entered.

**Gaps:** all 35 enlargement images are 1000px on the long edge; original painting medium, dimensions, year and individual descriptions were not published. Print preview sizes and advertised starting prices are preserved. Detailed price collection stopped at HTTP 429. Partial base calculations are unvalidated, not sale prices. ANC has no recoverable artwork image. No blog was found. The ANC fallback metadata image `/assets/fallback/default.jpg` was not fetched after throttling; it is a generic platform placeholder. CSS-only platform icons and dynamic merchandise mockups were not exhaustively mirrored.

See [navigation](navigation.md), [image manifest](images-manifest.json), [artwork data](artworks.json), and [plan](../docs/plan.md).

About Me
I'm Kevin,
I grew up in Anchorage, Alaska where I took an early interest in making art. I studied painting at the California College of the Arts, and have maintained a focus on my hometown as a muse for my work.
Growing up in Alaska, your first exposure to art tends to be depictions of Alaska's nature and wildlife. But there's more to life in Alaska, and the more I traveled outside Alaska, the more I came to appreciate the unique visual language of everyday life in my hometown. It's at once mundane, beautiful and for me, comforting.
My art business has been designated as a "Trusted Art Seller" with
The Art Storefronts Organization
, which means you can shop with confidence, and know that I stand behind the quality and value of my products.
Let's stay connected:

WHAT IS YOUR SHIPPING POLICY?
FOR PRINTS
We will choose the most cost-effective shipping carrier based on the items ordered and their destination. Shipping times range from 1-7 business days. You will receive a tracking number as soon as your order has been shipped.

**
Due to shipping requirements, pieces with a long side greater than 40" or a short side greater than 30" may incur additional charges.  Please see our Crate and Freight shipping policies below for more information.
Large Size Products and Crating Policies
Due to shipping requirements and the potential for damage, some products over 30" on the short side, or over 40" in length must be crated at an additional cost.
All additional Crate fees are included in the product total.
Crate for MetalPrints over 30x40 to 40x60 $15.00 (required)
Crate for MetalPrints over 40x60 $20.00 (required)
Products over 40" long that are shipped flat may require freight shipping,
We can provide freight quotes for these orders. Please allow additional time for the quote and freight delivery times.
Shipping Outside of the Continental US, Expedited Shipping, and Special Requests
APO shipping is charged full cost and sent via USPS.
Requests for shipping outside of the continental United States, other types of shipping, and expedited shipping will be charged at full cost.
We are not responsible for duties and taxes required for international shipping.
Shipping Quotes are available for large orders or orders shipping to international destinations. Please
contact us
with the details of what will be in the order and the address it will be shipped to.
WHAT IS YOUR POLICY ON RETURNS / EXCHANGES / REFUNDS?
We do not accept returns; all sales are FINAL.
We do our utmost to ensure that your prints are packaged carefully and arrive safely at their destination.
If your prints arrive damaged, please keep all packaging and
contact us
with your order number for further instructions.
Can I cancel my order?
All print sales are final. Please
contact us
as soon as possible if you need to cancel your order.
How long does it take to process a refund?
Processing a refund can take up to 7-10 business days.
How do I return my package?
For a refund or replacement, please contact us
here
.
Who to contact?
You can reach out at any time sending us a message
here.
HOW CAN I KEEP UP WITH YOUR NEW WORK?
We would love for you to be the first to know about any new work! An easy way to stay in the loop is by signing up for our
newsletter
!  You can also follow us on our social media pages as we are constantly posting about new work!

## Image provenance — every downloaded URL

Images retain server-delivered bytes; no upscaling. Hash suffixes prevent filename collisions. Artwork files use published titles; generic artwork variants were matched to identified enlargements by image comparison. Site branding and stock/platform assets are retained as references, not proposed new-site assets.
